==========================================================================================
2000 HRS Exit (Final Release)
ODBC Reference: [HRS2000x]
Perl script: CodebkD9.pl v5.043 Tue Mar  1  9:49:34 2005

Note to Users: This program is designed to create a codebook that matches the 
distribution dataset. Codebook content is derived from Surveycraft metadata.
If you have any questions or suggestions concerning codebook layout or content 
please bring them to the attention of the HRS staff at hrsquest@isr.umich.edu. 

Printing recommendation: Set margins (left/right/top/bottom) to .5"; print in 
portrait orientation using a mono-space 10-point font.
==========================================================================================

FSUBHH    1998 SUB-HOUSEHOLD IDENTIFIER
          Section: N            Level: Respondent         CAI Reference: Q9003
          Type: Character       Width: 1                  Decimals: 0
          ................................................................................
           1306         0. Original sample household - no split from divorce or
                           separation of spouses or partners
             14         1. Split household - one half of couple from SUBHH 0 and new
                           spouse or partner, if any
             13         2. Split household - other half of couple from SUBHH 0 and new
                           spouse or partner, if any
             15         3. 1st deceased respondent from a household
                        5. Split household - one half of couple from SUBHH 1 or 2
                        6. Split household - one half of couple from SUBHH 1 or 2
                        7. Reunited household - respondents from split household reunite

RPN_SP    2000 SPOUSE/PARTNER PERSON NUMBER
          Section: N            Level: Respondent         CAI Reference: Q9005
          Type: Character       Width: 3                  Decimals: 0

          User Note:  There are 42 R's with a valid RPN_SP who are not coupled in the
          2000 Exit.  Two reasons explain this occurrence: both members of a
          previously intact household are part of the 2000 Exit, or there was a
          legitimate split between the exit interview and their last core interview.
          We have included them in the data to allow linkage back to the spouse's
          interview.
          ................................................................................
            276       010. Spouse Person Number
             14       011. Spouse Person Number
            292       020. Spouse Person Number
              4       021. Spouse Person Number
             25       030. Spouse Person Number
             34       040. Spouse Person Number
            703     Blank. No Spouse at Death

R2323     N1x.OWNED HOME
          Section: N            Level: Respondent         CAI Reference: Q2323
          Type: Numeric         Width: 1                  Decimals: 0

          N1x.
          IF Q604 IS (NE1) AND Q514 IS (NE2)
                  Our records show that
                  [Q754-CS22Y31.R FIRST NAME]  owned a home
          ELSE Q604 IS (NE1) AND Q514 IS (2)
                  Our records show that you and
                  [Q754-CS22Y31.R FIRST NAME]  owned a home
          ELSE Q604 IS (1) AND Q514 IS (NE2)
                  Our records show that (he/she)
                  (and (his/her) husband/and (his/her) wife/and (his/her) partner/...)
          owned a home
          ELSE
                  Our records show that you and (he/she) owned a home
          END
          IF Q218 IS (NE0) AND Q219 IS (NE0)
                  in (Q48-PR48.HH1 PREV WAVE 1ST ADDRESS CITY/Q101-PR101.HH2 PREV WAVE
          1ST ADDRESS CITY) (Q49-PR49.HH1 PREV WAVE 1ST ADDRESS STATE/Q102-PR102.HH2
          PREV WAVE 1ST ADDRESS STATE) in
                  [Q218-PR218.PREV WAVE IW MONTH]
                  [Q219.] .
          ELSE
                  in (Q48-PR48.HH1 PREV WAVE 1ST ADDRESS CITY/Q101-PR101.HH2 PREV WAVE
          1ST ADDRESS CITY) (Q49-PR49.HH1 PREV WAVE 1ST ADDRESS STATE/Q102-PR102.HH2
          PREV WAVE 1ST ADDRESS STATE) in the last two years.
          END
                  Please, let me confirm, did
                  [Q754-CS22Y31.R FIRST NAME]  still own that home
                  when (he/she) died?

          User Note:  Variables R2323 - R2354 were part of the Estate Call-back
          effort.
          ................................................................................
            630         1. YES
             30         2. Records incorrect
             59         5. NO
              2         8. DK (Don't Know); NA (Not Ascertained)
                        9. RF (Refused)
            627     Blank. INAP (Inapplicable): [Q2311] IS (15); [Q2311] IS (16); [Q2311]
                           IS (18); [Q284:PR284] IS (NE 1)

R2430     N73x.HAPPENED TO ESTATE
          Section: N            Level: Respondent         CAI Reference: Q2430

                  The next questions are about
                  [Q754-CS22Y31.R FIRST NAME]'s assets
